[Sprite/Animation Information? 16 byte records]

Each line is loaded into an entire $E070,Y row for a given enemy.

00, 
01: ANDed with #$03FF, ASL'd 6 times, stored into $E080,Y.

01: LSR'd twice, ANDed with #$07, #$D5 added to determine sprite bank, storedi n $E082,Y.

04: ANDed with #$7F, added with and stored back into $E11A,Y. (Example: 86 becomes 6 and then 2)

And then, later:
00e532 lda $e074,x   
[7ee874] 
A:0084 X:0800 Y:0000 S:01f2 D:0000 DB:7e nvmxdIzc V: 95 H:1264
00e535 and #$007f
A:aa86 X:0800 Y:0000 S:01f2 D:0000 DB:7e NvmxdIzc V: 95 H:1272
00e538 clc
A:0006 X:0800 Y:0000 S:01f2 D:0000 DB:7e nvmxdIzc V: 95 H:1296
00e539 adc $e045,x
[7ee845]
A:0006 X:0800 Y:0000 S:01f2 D:0000 DB:7e nvmxdIzc V: 95 H:1310
00e53c eor #$ffff
A:0006 X:0800 Y:0000 S:01f2 D:0000 DB:7e nvmxdIzc V: 95 H:1356
00e53f sec
A:fff9 X:0800 Y:0000 S:01f2 D:0000 DB:7e NvmxdIzc V: 96 H:  16
00e540 adc $e11a,x
[7ee91a]
A:fff9 X:0800 Y:0000 S:01f2 D:0000 DB:7e NvmxdIzC V: 96 H:  30
00e543 adc $e022,x
[7ee822]
A:fffc X:0800 Y:0000 S:01f2 D:0000 DB:7e NvmxdIzc V: 96 H:  76
00e546 sta $06
[000006]
A:0069 X:0800 Y:0000 S:01f2 D:0000 DB:7e nvmxdIzC V: 96 H: 122

05,
06: 4CAA, starting point of a list of pointers in Bank 11.

07, 
08:

09, 
0A: 0000, AI offset from...wherever it starts.

0B, 
0C: 3546, starting point of a list of pointers in Bank 11.

0D, 
0E: 3546, starting point of a list of pointers in Bank 11.

Seiken Bin.:
0~3: Graphics
4: Shadow Pattern
5~8: Animation
09~0A: Attack algorithm
0B~0F:

[Table_HeadArmor]
;     Legend
;     SB: Stat Boost
;         0x40: Always Set
;         0x10: Strength
;         0x08: Agility
;         0x04: Constitution
;         0x02: Intelligence
;         0x01: Wisdom
;     DF: Defense
;     EV: Evasion
;     MD: Magic Defense
;     MV: Magic Evasion
;     EQ: Equippable By
;         0xE0: Everyone
;         0xA0: Randi & Popoie
;         0x60: Purim & Popoie
;         0x80: Randi
;         0x40: Purim
;         0x20: Popoie
;     EL: Elemental Resistance/Weakness (Dummied Out, but functional if you alter the table)
;     R1: Status Resist 1
;     R2: Status Resist 2
;     ??: ???
        SB DF EV MD MV EQ EL R1 R2 ??
        00 01 02 03 04 05 06 07 08 09

[CastSpellWithReflectLong]
D0/4C82:	0B      	PHD             ;Push Direct Page Register.
D0/4C83:	8B      	PHB             ;Push Data Bank Register.
D0/4C84:	08      	PHP             ;Push Processor Status Register.
D0/4C85:	225E39C0	JSR $C0395E     ;Jump to CleanseTargetingOffsetsLong.
D0/4C89:	BDFBE1		LDA $E1FB,X     ;Load [CHARACTER_TYPE, X] into Accumulator.
D0/4C8C:	100D +   	BPL $4C9B       ;Branch to CastSpellWithReflectLong_TestForWall if the target is not a player character.
D0/4C8E:	BD91E1		LDA $E191,X     ;Load [STATUS_EFFECTS_02, X] into Accumulator.
D0/4C91:	1008 +   	BPL $4C9B       ;Branch to CastSpellWithReflectLong_TestForWall if the target is not dead.
D0/4C93:	BDB1E1		LDA $E1B1,X     ;Load [MISC_FLAGS, X] into Accumulator.
D0/4C96:	0908		ORA #$08        ;Set 'Dead'.
D0/4C98:	9DB1E1    	STA $E1B1,X     ;Store result back into [MISC_FLAGS, X].

[CastSpellWithReflectLong_TestForWall]
D0/4C9B: +	BDB1E1  	LDA $E1B1,X     ;Load [MISC_FLAGS, X] into Accumulator.
D0/4C9E:	8940		BIT #$40        ;Test for 'Wall'.
D0/4CA0:	D011		BNE $4CB3       ;Branch to CastSpellWithReflectLong_TryReflectSpell if the target has Wall.

[CastSpellWithReflectLong_CastSpell]
D0/4CA2:	A485		LDY $85         ;Load {TempVar:TargetOffset} into Register Y.
D0/4CA4:	A683		LDX $83         ;Load {TempVar:CasterOffset} into Register X.
D0/4CA6:	22D4E6C8	JSR $C8E6D4     ;Jump to CastSpellLong.
D0/4CAA:	A900		LDA #$00        ;Load 0x00 into Accumulator.
D0/4CAC:	A20000    	LDX #$0000      ;Load 0x0000 into Register X.

[CastSpellWithReflectLong_Exit]
D0/4CAF:	28      	PLP             ;Pull Processor Status Register.
D0/4CB0:	AB      	PLB             ;Pull Data Bank Register.
D0/4CB1:	2B      	PLD             ;Pull Direct Page Register.
D0/4CB2:	6B      	RTL             ;Return Long.

[CastSpellWithReflectLong_TryReflectSpell]
D0/4CB3:	B970E1  	LDA $E170,Y     ;Load [CAST_SPELL_ID, Y] into Accumulator.
D0/4CB6:	C920		CMP #$20        ;Test to see if the spell is 'Mana Magic (Popoie)'.
D0/4CB8:	F0E8      	BEQ $4CA2       ;Branch to CastSpellWithReflectLong_CastSpell if the spell is 'Mana Magic (Popoie)'.
D0/4CBA:	C923		CMP #$23        ;Test to see if the spell is 'Mana Magic (Purim)'.
D0/4CBC:	F0E4		BEQ $4CA2       ;Branch to CastSpellWithReflectLong_CastSpell if the spell is 'Mana Magic (Purim)'.
D0/4CBE:	C926		CMP #$26        ;Test to see if the spell is 'Dispel Magic'.
D0/4CC0:	F0E0		BEQ $4CA2       ;Branch to CastSpellWithReflectLong_CastSpell if the spell is 'Dispel Magic'.
D0/4CC2:	A20300		LDX #$0003      ;Load 0x0003 into Register X.
D0/4CC5:	A581		LDA $81         ;Load {TempVar:SpellTargetIndex} into Accumulator.
D0/4CC7:	1A			INC A           ;Increment Accumulator.

[CastSpellWithReflectLong_FindReflectTarget_LoopStart]
D0/4CC8: -	D978E1  	CMP $E178,Y     ;Compare {TempVar:SpellTargetIndex} against [REFLECT_SPELL_TARGET, Y].
D0/4CCB:	F006 +   	BEQ $4CD3       ;Branch to CastSpellWithReflectLong_UpdateTargetWallState if they match.
D0/4CCD:	C8      	INY             ;Increment Register Y.
D0/4CCE:	CA      	DEX             ;Decrement Register X.
D0/4CCF:	D0F7 -   	BNE $4CC8       ;Branch to CastSpellWithReflectLong_FindReflectTarget_LoopStart if there are still targets to check.
D0/4CD1:	80CF    	BRA $4CA2       ;Branch to CastSpellWithReflectLong_CastSpell.

[CastSpellWithReflectLong_UpdateTargetWallState]
D0/4CD3: +	A485    	LDY $85         ;Load {TempVar:TargetOffset} into Register Y.
D0/4CD5:	A683    	LDX $83         ;Load {TempVar:CasterOffset} into Register X.
D0/4CD7:	B9B8E1  	LDA $E1B8,Y     ;Load [WALL_USE_COUNTER, Y] into Accumulator.
D0/4CDA:	3A      	DEC A           ;Decrement [WALL_USE_COUNTER, Y].
D0/4CDB:	99B8E1  	STA $E1B8,Y     ;Store result back into [WALL_USE_COUNTER, Y].
D0/4CDE:	D008 +   	BNE $4CE8       ;Branch to CastSpellWithReflectLong_GetReflectTargetBaseOffset if the target still has Wall uses left.
D0/4CE0:	B9B1E1  	LDA $E1B1,Y     ;Load [MISC_FLAGS, Y] into Accumulator.
D0/4CE3:	29BF  		AND #$BF        ;Remove 'Wall'.
D0/4CE6:	99B1E1    	STA $E1B1,Y     ;Store result back into [MISC_FLAGS, Y].

[CastSpellWithReflectLong_GetReflectTargetBaseOffset]
D0/4CE8: +	B9FBE1  	LDA $E1FB,Y     ;Load [CHARACTER_TYPE, Y] into Accumulator.
D0/4CEB:	3005 +   	BMI $4CF2       ;Branch to CastSpellWithReflectLong_GetReflectTargetBaseOffset_EnemyOffset if the target is a player character.
D0/4CED:	A20000  	LDX #$0000      ;Load 0x0000 into Register X. (Reflect on a player character)
D0/4CF0:	8003    	BRA $4CF5       ;Branch to CastSpellWithReflectLong_GetRandomReflectTarget.

[CastSpellWithReflectLong_GetReflectTargetBaseOffset_EnemyOffset]
D0/4CF2: +	A20006  	LDX #$0600      ;Load 0x0600 into Register X. (Reflect on an enemy)

[CastSpellWithReflectLong_GetRandomReflectTarget]
D0/4CF5:	A903  		LDA #$03        ;Load 0x03 into Accumulator.
D0/4CF7:	85B2    	STA $B2         ;Store 0x03 into {TempVar:LoopCounter}.
D0/4CF9:	229C38C0  	JSR C0389C      ;Jump to GetRandomNumberLong.
D0/4CFD:	C220    	REP #$20        ;Enable 16-Bit Accumulator.
D0/4CFF:	86D0    	STX $D0         ;Store Register X into {TempVar:ReflectTargetOffset}.
D0/4D01:	290300  	AND #$0003      ;Remove everything but the bottom two bits.
D0/4D04:	0A      	ASL A           ;Multiply By 2.
D0/4D05:	0A      	ASL A           ;Multiply By 2.
D0/4D06:	0A      	ASL A           ;Multiply By 2.
D0/4D07:	85D4    	STA $D4         ;Store result into {TempVar:RandomReflectOffsetIndex}.
D0/4D09:	B902E0  	LDA $E002,Y     ;Load [X_COORDINATE, Y] into Accumulator.
D0/4D0C:	85AC    	STA $AC         ;Store [X_COORDINATE, Y] into {TempVar:OriginalTargetXCoordinate}.
D0/4D0E:	B904E0  	LDA $E004,Y     ;Load [Y_COORDINATE, Y] into Accumulator.
D0/4D11:	85AE    	STA $AE         ;Store [Y_COORDINATE, Y] into {TempVar:OriginalTargetYCoordinate}.
D0/4D13:	E220    	SEP #$20        ;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.

[CastSpellWithReflectLong_GetRandomReflectTarget_LoopStart]
D0/4D15: -	C220    	REP #$20        ;Enable 16-Bit Accumulator.
D0/4D17:	A5D4    	LDA $D4         ;Load {TempVar:RandomReflectOffsetIndex} into Accumulator.
D0/4D19:	AA      	TAX             ;Load {TempVar:RandomReflectOffsetIndex} into Register X.
D0/4D1A:	BF7E4DD0	LDA $D04D7E,X   ;Load an offset from DataTable_RandomReflectOffsets.
D0/4D1E:	18      	CLC             ;Clear Carry Flag.
D0/4D1F:	65D0    	ADC $D0         ;Add the random offset to {TempVar:ReflectTargetOffset}.
D0/4D21:	AA      	TAX             ;Transfer the randomly created offset to Register X.
D0/4D22:	E220    	SEP #$20        ;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
D0/4D24:	BD00E0  	LDA $E000,X     ;Load [MASTER_ACTIVATOR, X] into Accumulator.
D0/4D27:	C901    	CMP #$01        ;Test to see if the sprite slot is active.
D0/4D29:	D044 +   	BNE $4D6F       ;Branch to CastSpellWithReflectLong_GetRandomReflectTarget_ContinueSearch if the sprite slot is inactive.
D0/4D2B:	BD91E1  	LDA $E191,X     ;Load [STATUS_EFFECTS_02, X] into Accumulator.
D0/4D2E:	303F +   	BMI $4D6F       ;Branch to CastSpellWithReflectLong_GetRandomReflectTarget_ContinueSearch if the reflect target is dead.
D0/4D30:	BDFBE1  	LDA $E1FB,X     ;Load [CHARACTER_TYPE, X] into Accumulator.
D0/4D33:	8930    	BIT #$30        ;Test for 'Crystal Orb' and 'Treasure Chest'.
D0/4D35:	D038 +    	BNE $4D6F       ;Branch to CastSpellWithReflectLong_GetRandomReflectTarget_ContinueSearch if the reflect target is a Crystal Orb or Treasure Chest.
D0/4D37:	ADED00  	LDA $00ED       ;Load [CURRENT_MAP_STYLE] into Accumulator.
D0/4D3A:	1033 +   	BPL $4D6F       ;Branch to CastSpellWithReflectLong_GetRandomReflectTarget_ContinueSearch if the spell was cast on a non-combat map.
D0/4D3C:	C220    	REP #$20        ;Enable 16-Bit Accumulator.
D0/4D3E:	BD02E0  	LDA $E002,X     ;Load [X_COORDINATE, X] into Accumulator.
D0/4D41:	38      	SEC             ;Set Carry Flag.
D0/4D42:	E5AC    	SBC $AC         ;Subtract {TempVar:OriginalTargetXCoordinate} from the reflect target's X-Coordinate.
D0/4D44:	1004 ++   	BPL $4D4A       ;Branch to CastSpellWithReflectLong_GetRandomReflectTarget_GetYDelta if the delta is positive.
D0/4D46:	49FFFF  	EOR #$FFFF      ;Invert the X-Coordinate delta.
D0/4D49:	1A      	INC A           ;Increment the X-Coordinate delta.

[CastSpellWithReflectLong_GetRandomReflectTarget_GetYDelta]
D0/4D4A: ++	85A8    	STA $A8         ;Store the result into {TempVar:XCoodinateDelta}.
D0/4D4C:	BD04E0  	LDA $E004,X     ;Load [Y_COORDINATE, X] into Accumulator.
D0/4D4F:	38      	SEC             ;Set Carry Flag.
D0/4D50:	E5AE    	SBC $AE         ;Subtract {TempVar:OriginalTargetYCoordinate} from the reflect target's Y-Coordinate.
D0/4D52:	1004 ++   	BPL $4D58       ;Branch to CastSpellWithReflectLong_ReflectSpell if the delta is positive.
D0/4D54:	49FFFF  	EOR #$FFFF      ;Invert the Y-Coordinate delta.
D0/4D57:	1A      	INC A           ;Increment the Y-Coordinate delta.

[CastSpellWithReflectLong_ReflectSpell]
D0/4D58: ++	85AA    	STA $AA         ;Store the result into {TempVar:YCoodinateDelta}.
D0/4D5A:	18      	CLC             ;Clear Carry Flag.
D0/4D5B:	65A8    	ADC $A8         ;Add {TempVar:XCoodinateDelta} to {TempVar:YCoodinateDelta}.
D0/4D5D:	C9F000  	CMP #$00F0      ;Compare the total coordinate delta against 0x00F0.
D0/4D60:	E220    	SEP #$20        ;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
D0/4D62:	B00B +   	BCS $4D6F       ;Branch to CastSpellWithReflectLong_GetRandomReflectTarget_ContinueSearch if the reflect target is to far from the original target.
D0/4D64:	DA      	PHX             ;Push Register X.
D0/4D65:	225F59C0	JSR $C0595F     ;Jump to LoadWallReflectMessageLong.
D0/4D69:	FA      	PLX             ;Pull Register X.
D0/4D6A:	A9FF    	LDA #$FF        ;Load 0xFF into Accumulator. (Spell was successfully reflected)
D0/4D6C:	4CAF4C  	JMP $4CAF       ;Jump to CastSpellWithReflectLong_Exit.

[CastSpellWithReflectLong_GetRandomReflectTarget_ContinueSearch]
D0/4D6F: +	E6CD    	INC $CD         ;Increment {TempVar:CharacterOffsetH}.
D0/4D71:	E6CD    	INC $CD         ;Increment {TempVar:CharacterOffsetH}.
D0/4D73:	E6D4    	INC $D4         ;Increment {TempVar:RandomReflectOffsetIndex}.
D0/4D75:	E6D4    	INC $D4         ;Increment {TempVar:RandomReflectOffsetIndex}.
D0/4D77:	C6B2    	DEC $B2         ;Decrement {TempVar:LoopCounter}.
D0/4D79:	D09A -    	BNE $4D15       ;Branch to CastSpellWithReflectLong_GetRandomReflectTarget_LoopStart if all three slots have not been checked.
D0/4D7B:	4CA24C  	JMP $4CA2       ;Jump to CastSpellWithReflectLong_CastSpell.

[DataTable_RandomReflectOffsets]
D0/4D7E:	0000 [00: ]
D0/4D80:	0002 [01: ]
D0/4D82:	0004 [02: ]

D0/4D84:	0000 [03: Unreachable]

D0/4D86:	0002 [04: ]
D0/4D88:	0004 [05: ]
D0/4D8A:	0000 [06: ]

D0/4D8C:	0000 [07: ]
D0/4D8E:	0004 [08: ]
D0/4D90:	0002 [09: ]

D0/4D92:	0000 [0A: Unreachable]

D0/4D94:	0000 [0C: ]
D0/4D96:	0002 [0D: ]
D0/4D98:	0004 [0E: ]

D0/4D9A:	0000 [0F: Unreachable]
D0/4D9C:	0000 [10: Unreachable]

[PrepareToCastSpellLong]
104D9E: 0B              PHD             ;Push Direct Page Register.
104D9F: 8B              PHB             ;Push Data Bank Register.
104DA0: 08              PHP             ;Push Processor Status Register.
104DA1: 225E39C0        JSR $C0395E     ;Jump to CleanseTargetingOffsetsLong.
104DA5: A683            LDX $83         ;Load {TempVar:CasterOffset} into Register X.
104DA7: A9FF            LDA #$FF        ;Load 0xFF into Accumulator.
104DA9: 9D72E1          STA $E172,X     ;Store 0xFF into [PRIMARY_SPELL_TARGET, X].
104DAC: 9D73E1          STA $E173,X     ;Store 0xFF into [SECONDARY_SPELL_TARGET, X].
104DAF: 9D74E1          STA $E174,X     ;Store 0xFF into [TERTIARY_SPELL_TARGET, X].
104DB2: 206E4E          JSR $4E6E       ;Jump to LoadSpellElementAndExperience.
104DB5: A683            LDX $83         ;Load {TempVar:CasterOffset} into Register X.
104DB7: 20AC4E          JSR $4EAC       ;Jump to TryCastSuperMagic.
104DBA: A683            LDX $83         ;Load {TempVar:CasterOffset} into Register X.
104DBC: BD70E1          LDA $E170,X     ;Load [CAST_SPELL_ID, X] into Accumulator.
104DBF: 8F024200        STA $004202     ;Store [CAST_SPELL_ID, X] into [WRMPYA].
104DC3: A940            LDA #$40        ;Load 0x40 into Accumulator.
104DC5: 8F034200        STA $004203     ;Store 0x40 into [WRMPYB].
104DC9: 5C693FC0        JMP $C03F69     ;Jump to CastSpellPrepLong.

[TryCalculateSpellExperienceLong]
104DCD: B9FBE1          LDA $E1FB,Y     ;Load [CHARACTER_TYPE, Y] into Accumulator.
104DD0: 3001            BMI $4DD3       ;Branch to CalculateSpellExperienceLong if the caster is a player character.
104DD2: 6B              RTL             ;Return Long.

[CalculateSpellExperienceLong]
;     Max Spell Level = Current Sealed Seed Level
;     Casting a spell grants (9 - CurrentLevel) experience.
;     Divided by 2 if your in a non-combat zone.
104DD3: 206E4E          JSR $4E6E       ;Jump to LoadSpellElementAndExperience.
104DD6: ADFCCF          LDA $CFFC       ;Load [FLAG_SEALED_MANA_SEEDS] into Accumulator.
104DD9: C908            CMP #$08        ;Compare [FLAG_SEALED_MANA_SEEDS] with 0x08.
104DDB: D001            BNE $4DDE       ;Branch to CalculateSpellExperienceLong_Begin if [FLAG_SEALED_MANA_SEEDS] is not 0x08.
104DDD: 1A              INC A           ;Increment the sealed mana seeds value.

[CalculateSpellExperienceLong_Begin]
104DDE: 85A6            STA $A6         ;Store result in {TempVar:MaxSpellLevel}.
104DE0: A59D            LDA $9D         ;Load {TempVar:CurrentSpellLevel} into Accumulator.
104DE2: C5A6            CMP $A6         ;Compare {TempVar:CurrentSpellLevel} with {TempVar:MaxSpellLevel}.
104DE4: 9006            BCC $4DEC       ;If {TempVar:CurrentSpellLevel} is less then {TempVar:MaxSpellLevel} then branch to CalculateSpellExperienceLong_Begin_GetExpIncrease.
104DE6: A59E            LDA $9E         ;Load {TempVar:CurrentElementalExperence} into Accumulator.
104DE8: 85A8            STA $A8         ;Store {TempVar:CurrentElementalExperence} in {TempVar:NewElementalExperence}.
104DEA: 8071            BRA $4E5D       ;Branch to CalculateSpellExperienceLong_Exit.

[CalculateSpellExperienceLong_Begin_GetExpIncrease]
104DEC: A909            LDA #$09        ;Load 0x09 into Accumulator.
104DEE: 38              SEC             ;Set Carry Flag.
104DEF: E59D            SBC $9D         ;Subtract {TempVar:CurrentSpellLevel} from 0x09.
104DF1: 85AC            STA $AC         ;Store result in {TempVar:ExpForCastingSpell}.
104DF3: ADED00          LDA $00ED       ;Load [CURRENT_MAP_STYLE] into Accumulator.
104DF6: 3002            BMI $4DFA       ;Branch to CalculateSpellExperienceLong_Begin_UpdateCasterExperience if it's a combat map.
104DF8: 46AC            LSR $AC         ;Otherwise divide {TempVar:ExpForCastingSpell} by 2.

[CalculateSpellExperienceLong_Begin_UpdateCasterExperience]
104DFA: B977E1          LDA $E177,Y     ;Load [CAST_SPELL_ELEMENT, Y] into Accumulator.
104DFD: 85CC            STA $CC         ;Store it into {TempElementIndex}.
104DFF: A4CC            LDY $CC         ;Load {TempVar:ElementIndex} into Register Y.
104E01: B9D8E1          LDA $E1D8,Y     ;Load [CURRENT_EXPERENCE_ELEMENTAL, Y] into Accumulator.
104E04: 859E            STA $9E         ;Store [CURRENT_EXPERENCE_ELEMENTAL, Y] into {TempVar:CurrentElementalExperence}.
104E06: 18              CLC             ;Clear Carry Flag.
104E07: 65AC            ADC $AC         ;Add {TempVar:ExpForCastingSpell} to {TempVar:CurrentElementalExperence}.
104E09: 85A8            STA $A8         ;Store result into {TempVar:NewElementalExperence}.
104E0B: C964            CMP #$64        ;Compare {TempVar:NewElementalExperence} with 0x64(100).
104E0D: 904E            BCC $4E5D       ;If {TempVar:NewElementalExperence} is less than 100 then branch to CalculateSpellExperienceLong_Exit.
104E0F: A908            LDA #$08        ;Load 0x08 into Accumulator.
104E11: C59D            CMP $9D         ;Compare {TempVar:CurrentSpellLevel} with 0x08.
104E13: D007            BNE $4E1C       ;Branch to CalculateSpellExperienceLong_Begin_IncreaseElementalLevel if the current spell level is < 0x08.
104E15: A963            LDA #$63        ;Load 0x63(99) into Accumulator.
104E17: 85A8            STA $A8         ;Store 0x63(99) into {TempVar:NewElementalExperence}.
104E19: 4C5D4E          JMP $4E5D       ;Jump to CalculateSpellExperienceLong_Exit.

[CalculateSpellExperienceLong_Begin_IncreaseElementalLevel]
104E1C: 64A8            STZ $A8         ;Store Zero into {TempVar:NewElementalExperence}.
104E1E: E69D            INC $9D         ;Increment {TempVar:CurrentSpellLevel}.
104E20: A483            LDY $83         ;Load {TempVar:CasterOffset} into Accumulator.
104E22: B977E1          LDA $E177,Y     ;Load [CAST_SPELL_ELEMENT, Y] into Accumulator.
104E25: 8901            BIT #$01        ;Test for Lower Bit Elemental.
104E27: D010            BNE $4E39       ;Branch to CalculateSpellExperienceLong_Begin_IncreaseElementalLevel_LowNibbleIncrease if that's the one that level'd up.
104E29: 4A              LSR A           ;Divide [CAST_SPELL_ELEMENT, Y] by 2.
104E2A: 85CC            STA $CC         ;Store it into {TempElementIndex}.
104E2C: A4CC            LDY $CC         ;Load {TempVar:ElementIndex} into Register Y.
104E2E: B9C4E1          LDA $E1C4,Y     ;Load [SKILL_LEVEL_ELEMENTAL, Y] into Accumulator.
104E31: 18              CLC             ;Clear Carry Flag.
104E32: 6910            ADC #$10        ;Add 0x10 to [SKILL_LEVEL_ELEMENTAL, Y].
104E34: 99C4E1          STA $E1C4,Y     ;Store result back into [SKILL_LEVEL_ELEMENTAL, Y].
104E37: 800C            BRA $4E45       ;Branch to CalculateSpellExperienceLong_Begin_DisplayLevelUpMessage.

[CalculateSpellExperienceLong_Begin_IncreaseElementalLevel_LowNibbleIncrease]
104E39: 4A              LSR A           ;Divide [CAST_SPELL_ELEMENT, Y] by 2.
104E3A: 85CC            STA $CC         ;Store it into {TempVar:ElementIndex}.
104E3C: A4CC            LDY $CC         ;Load {TempVar:ElementIndex} into Register Y.
104E3E: B9C4E1          LDA $E1C4,Y     ;Load [SKILL_LEVEL_ELEMENTAL, Y] into Accumulator.
104E41: 1A              INC A           ;Increment [SKILL_LEVEL_ELEMENTAL, Y].
104E42: 99C4E1          STA $E1C4,Y     ;Store result back into [SKILL_LEVEL_ELEMENTAL, Y].

[CalculateSpellExperienceLong_Begin_DisplayLevelUpMessage]
104E45: A5A8            LDA $A8         ;Load {TempVar:NewElementalExperence} into Accumulator.
104E47: 48              PHA             ;Push Accumulator.
104E48: DA              PHX             ;Push Register X.
104E49: 5A              PHY             ;Push Register Y.
104E4A: A683            LDX $83         ;Load {TempVar:CasterOffset} into Register X.
104E4C: 22755BC0        JSR $C05B75     ;Jump to LoadMagicLevelUpMessageLong.
104E50: A9CF            LDA #$CF        ;Load 0xCF into Accumulator.
104E52: 228939C0        JSR $C03989     ;Jump to PlaySoundEffectLong.
104E56: 7A              PLY             ;Pull Register Y.
104E57: 84CC            STY $CC         ;Store Register Y into {TempVar:ElementIndex}.
104E59: FA              PLX             ;Pull Register X.
104E5A: 68              PLA             ;Pull Accumulator.
104E5B: 85A8            STA $A8         ;Store Accumulator into {TempVar:NewElementalExperence}.

[CalculateSpellExperienceLong_Exit]
104E5D: A483            LDY $83         ;Load {TempVar:CasterOffset} into Register Y.
104E5F: B977E1          LDA $E177,Y     ;Load [CAST_SPELL_ELEMENT, Y] into Accumulator.
104E62: 85CC            STA $CC         ;Store [CAST_SPELL_ELEMENT, Y] into {TempVar:ElementIndex}.
104E64: A4CC            LDY $CC         ;Load {TempVar:ElementIndex} into Register Y.
104E66: A5A8            LDA $A8         ;Load {TempVar:NewElementalExperence} into Accumulator.
104E68: 99D8E1          STA $E1D8,Y     ;Store {TempVar:NewElementalExperence} into [CURRENT_EXPERENCE_ELEMENTAL, Y].
104E6B: A483            LDY $83         ;Load {TempVar:CasterOffset} into Register Y.
104E6D: 6B              RTL             ;Return Long.

[LoadSpellElementAndExperience]
104E6E: B9FBE1          LDA $E1FB,Y     ;Load [CHARACTER_TYPE, Y] into Accumulator.
104E71: 300B            BMI $4E7E       ;If it's a player character branch to LoadSpellElementAndExperience_Player.
104E73: B9C4E1          LDA $E1C4,Y     ;Load [SKILL_LEVEL_ELEMENTAL, Y] into Accumulator.
104E76: 859D            STA $9D         ;Store [SKILL_LEVEL_ELEMENTAL, Y] into {TempVar:CurrentSpellLevel}.
104E78: B9D8E1          LDA $E1D8,Y     ;Load [CURRENT_EXPERIENCE_ELEMENTAL, Y] into Accumulator.
104E7B: 859E            STA $9E         ;Store [CURRENT_EXPERIENCE_ELEMENTAL, Y] in {TempVar:CurrentElementalExperence}.
104E7D: 60              RTS             ;Return.

[LoadSpellElementAndExperience_Player]
104E7E: A483            LDY $83         ;Load {TempVar:CasterOffset} into Register Y.
104E80: 84CC            STY $CC         ;Store {TempVar:CasterOffset} into {TempVar:CasterOffsetRedux}.
104E82: B977E1          LDA $E177,Y     ;Load [CAST_SPELL_ELEMENT, Y] into Accumulator.
104E85: 85CC            STA $CC         ;Store [CAST_SPELL_ELEMENT, Y] into {TempVar:ElementIndex}.
104E87: A4CC            LDY $CC         ;Load {TempVar:ElementIndex} into Register Y.
104E89: B9D8E1          LDA $E1D8,Y     ;Load [CURRENT_EXPERENCE_ELEMENTAL, Y] into Accumulator.
104E8C: 859E            STA $9E         ;Store [CURRENT_EXPERENCE_ELEMENTAL, Y] into {TempVar:CurrentElementalExperence}.
104E8E: 46CC            LSR $CC         ;Divide {TempVar:ElementIndex} by 2.
104E90: 08              PHP             ;Push Processor Status Register.
104E91: A4CC            LDY $CC         ;Load {TempVar:ElementIndex} into Register Y.
104E93: B9C4E1          LDA $E1C4,Y     ;Load [SKILL_LEVEL_ELEMENTAL, Y] into Accumulator.
104E96: 85D0            STA $D0         ;Store [SKILL_LEVEL_ELEMENTAL, Y] into {TempVar:ElementalSkillLevel}.
104E98: 28              PLP             ;Pull Processor Status Register.
104E99: B008            BCS $4EA3       ;Branch to LoadSpellElementAndExperience_Player_Exit if the elemental level is stored in the low bits.
104E9B: 46D0            LSR $D0         ;Shift {TempVar:ElementalSkillLevel} right.
104E9D: 46D0            LSR $D0         ;Shift {TempVar:ElementalSkillLevel} right.
104E9F: 46D0            LSR $D0         ;Shift {TempVar:ElementalSkillLevel} right.
104EA1: 46D0            LSR $D0         ;Shift {TempVar:ElementalSkillLevel} right.

[LoadSpellElementAndExperience_Player_Exit]
104EA3: A5D0            LDA $D0         ;Load {TempVar:ElementalSkillLevel} into Accumulator.
104EA5: 290F            AND #$0F        ;Drop the high bits.
104EA7: 859D            STA $9D         ;Store result in {TempVar:CurrentSpellLevel}.
104EA9: A483            LDY $83         ;Load {TempVar:CasterOffset} into Register Y.
104EAB: 60              RTS             ;Return.

[TryCastSuperMagic]
; So bosses are explictly coded to be able to cast super magic.
; However none of them can since their spell experience is always zero.
104EAC: BDFBE1          LDA $E1FB,X     ;Load [CHARACTER_TYPE, X] into Accumulator.
104EAF: 89C0            BIT #$C0        ;Test for 'Boss' & 'Player Character'.
104EB1: D006            BNE $4EB9       ;Branch to TryCastSuperMagic_Core if the caster is the player or a boss.

[TryCastSuperMagic_Exit]
104EB3: A59D            LDA $9D         ;Load {TempVar:CurrentSpellLevel} into Accumulator.
104EB5: 9D71E1          STA $E171,X     ;Store {TempVar:CurrentSpellLevel} into [CAST_SPELL_LEVEL, X].
104EB8: 60              RTS             ;Return.

[TryCastSuperMagic_Core]
104EB9: A59D            LDA $9D         ;Load {TempVar:CurrentSpellLevel} into Accumulator.
104EBB: C908            CMP #$08        ;Compare {TempVar:CurrentSpellLevel} against 0x08.
104EBD: D00D            BNE $4ECC       ;Branch to TryCastSuperMagic_Core_Exit if the spell level is not Level 8.
104EBF: 229C38C0        JSR $C0389C     ;Jump to GetRandomNumberLong.
104EC3: 4A              LSR A           ;Divide the random number by 2.
104EC4: 1A              INC A           ;Increment the random number.
104EC5: C59E            CMP $9E         ;Compare the random number against {TempVar:CurrentElementalExperence}.
104EC7: 90EA            BCC $4EB3       ;If the random number is less then {TempVar:CurrentElementalExperience} then branch to TryCastSuperMagic_Exit.
104EC9: A59D            LDA $9D         ;Load {TempVar:CurrentSpellLevel} into Accumulator.
104ECB: 3A              DEC A           ;Decrement {TempVar:CurrentSpellLevel}.

[TryCastSuperMagic_Core_Exit]
104ECC: 9D71E1          STA $E171,X     ;Store {TempVar:CurrentSpellLevel} into [CAST_SPELL_LEVEL, X].
104ECF: 60              RTS             ;Return.

[DataTable_BossWeapons]
; Used by the boss loader routine at C0/45D6.
; For fucks sake, this table and the graphics table overlap.
; Ends up not mattering since the last graphics row is unused and so is the monster affinity byte, but ffs.

[PlaySoundEffectLong]
10CEDF: E220            SEP #$20        ;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
10CEE1: 8D011E          STA $1E01       ;Store Accumulator into [MUSIC_ID(High)].
10CEE4: A902            LDA #$02        ;Load 0x02 into Accumulator.
10CEE6: 8D001E          STA $1E00       ;Store 0x02 into [MUSIC_ID(Low)].
10CEE9: A90F            LDA #$0F        ;Load 0x0F into Accumulator.
10CEEB: 8D021E          STA $1E02       ;Store 0x0F into [MUSIC_FADE_IN_DELAY].
10CEEE: 9C031E          STZ $1E03       ;Store 0x0F into [MUSIC_VOLUME].
10CEF1: 220400C3        JSR $C30004     ;
10CEF5: 28              PLP             ;Pull Processor Status Register.
10CEF6: 68              PLA             ;Pull Accumulator.
10CEF7: 6B              RTL             ;Return Long.

;[FUNCTION_PLAY_SELECT_TARGET_SOUND_EFFECT 0x10CEF8-0x10CEFF]
10CEF8: 48              PHA             ;Push Accumulator.
10CEF9: 08              PHP             ;Push Processor Status Register.
10CEFA: E220            SEP #$20        ;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.
10CEFC: A90C            LDA #$0C        ;Load 0x0C into Accumulator. (Select Target Sound Effect)
10CEFE: 80DF            BRA $CEDF       ;Branch to PlaySoundEffectLong.

[FUNCTION_LOAD_RING_MENU_ICON 0x10D263-0x10D274]
D0/D263:	DA      	PHX 		;Push Register X.
D0/D264:	0A      	ASL A		;Multiply Accumulator By 2.
D0/D265:	18      	CLC 		;Clear Carry Flag.
D0/D266:	6D2818  	ADC $1828	;Add [ACTIVE_RING_ICON_POINTER] to Accumulator.
D0/D269:	AA      	TAX 		;Transfer the pointer to Register X.
D0/D26A:	BF60FDD8	LDA $D8FD60,X	;Load {RingIcon} & {RingPalette} into Accumulator.
D0/D26E:	990000  	STA $0000,Y	;Store into [RING_MENU_TILE_ID_SLOT, Y] & [RING_MENU_TILE_PALETTE_ID_SLOT, Y].
D0/D271:	C8      	INY 		;Increment Register Y.
D0/D272:	C8      	INY  		;Increment Register Y.
D0/D273:	FA      	PLX 		;Pull Register x.
D0/D274:	60      	RTS		;Return.

D0/D275:	2079D2  	JSR $D279

D0/D27A:	C230    	REP #$30	;Enable 16-Bit Accumulator & Index Registers.

D0/D27D:	48      	PHA 		;Push Accumulator.
D0/D27E:	DA      	PHX 		;Push Register X.

D0/D286:	E220    	SEP #$20	;Disable 16-Bit Accumulator.

D0/D28D:	BB      	TYX 		;Transfer Register Y to Register X.
